## Corpus Check
- 5710 files · ~4,495,682 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 60825 nodes · 107561 edges · 4123 communities (3533 shown, 590 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1098 edges (avg confidence: 0.68)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `0c40837e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
